set cli terminal width <1-500>
set cli terminal height <1-500>
set clock date <value> time <value>
set system setting url-database <brightcloud|paloaltonetworks>
set system setting default-wildfire-server <value>
set system setting mp-memory-monitor enable yes
set system setting mp-memory-monitor enable no
set system setting target device-group <value>
set system setting target template name <value> vsys <value>
set system setting target none
set system setting correlation enable yes
set system setting correlation enable no
set system setting paloalto-networks-service-proxy on
set system setting paloalto-networks-service-proxy off
set system nfs dynamic-logging-partition threshold <3-999999>
set max-num-images count <2-64>
